`${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n14\t\n15\tconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n16\t  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n17\t  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n18\t  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n19\t  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n20\t\n21\t  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n22\t\n23\t  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n24\t    contractName: \"USDC\",\n25\t    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n26\t    args: [connectedAddress],\n27\t  });\n28\t\n29\t  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n30\t    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n31\t    eventName: \"Paid\",\n32\t    fromBlock: 0n,\n33\t    watch: true,\n34\t  });\n35\t\n36\t  const handleApprove = async () => {\n37\t    try {\n38\t      await writeContractAsync({\n39\t        address: USDC.address,\n40\t        abi: USDC.abi,\n41\t        functionName: \"approve\",\n42\t        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n43\t      });\n44\t    } catch (e) {\n45\t      console.error(e);\n46\t    }\n47\t  };\n48\t\n49\t  const handlePay = async () => {\n50\t    try {\n51\t      await writeContractAsync({\n52\t        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n53\t        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n54\t        functionName: \"pay\",\n55\t        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n56\t      });\n57\t      setAmount(\"\");\n58\t      setMemo(\"\");\n59\t    } catch (e) {\n60\t      console.error(e);\n61\t    }\n62\t  };\n63\t\n64\t  return (\n65\t    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n66\t      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n67\t        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n68\t        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n69\t\n70\t        {!isConnected ? (\n71\t          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n72\t        ) : (\n73\t          <>\n74\t            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n75\t              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n76\t            </p>\n77\t\n78\t            <input\n79\t              type=\"text\"\n80\t              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n81\t              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n82\t              value={recipient}\n83\t              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n84\t            />\n85\t            <input\n86\t              type=\"number\"\n87\t              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n88\t              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n89\t              value={amount}\n90\t              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n91\t            />\n92\t            <textarea\n93\t              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n94\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n95\t              value={memo}\n96\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n97\t            />\n98\t\n99\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n100\t              <button\n101\t                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n102\t                disabled={isPending}\n103\t                onClick={handleApprove}\n104\t              >\n105\t                Approve\n106\t              </button>\n107\t              <button\n108\t                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n109\t                disabled={isPending}\n110\t                onClick={handlePay}\n111\t              >\n112\t                Send Payout\n113\t              </button>\n114\t            </div>\n115\t          </>\n116\t        )}\n117\t\n118\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n119\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n120\t          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n121\t            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n122\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n123\t              {\" paid \"}\n124\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n125\t              {\" \"}\n126\t              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n127\t              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n128\t            </div>\n129\t          ))}\n130\t          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n131\t        </div>\n132\t      </div>\n133\t    </div>\n134\t  );\n135\t};\n136\t\n137\texport default PayoutsPage;\n138\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"363d12fc-c46a-47cb-877b-d72987e759e8","uuid":"4098986f-bb43-469d-8453-94c6e1c14065","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:29:18.108Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152857Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\nconst USDC = deployedContracts[8453].USDC;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":138,"startLine":1,"totalLines":138}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM86bh2r2KEtQva1y5eV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XoWLKhEksTnNbqvKrGm9aS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152857Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// USDC uses 6 decimals\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: sendPayout, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Payouts\" });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // parseUnits throws on malformed input (e.g. \"1.2.3\"), so guard it\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = parsedAmount !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await approveUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount] });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await sendPayout({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n      <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n      <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n      {!isConnected ? (\n        <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <p className=\"mb-4\">\n            Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n          </p>\n\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={recipient}\n            onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <textarea\n            placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n            value={memo}\n            onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n          />\n\n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n              disabled={isApproving || !needsApproval}\n              onClick={handleApprove}\n            >\n              {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Approve\"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n              disabled={isPaying || needsApproval || !hasValidAmount}\n              onClick={handlePay}\n            >\n              {isPaying ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Send Payout\"}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n          {needsApproval && <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mt-2\">Approve USDC spending before sending this payout.</p>}\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n        {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n          <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"card bg-base-200 p-3\">\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1\">\n              <Address address={payout.args.from} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n              <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n              <Address address={payout.args.to} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n              <span className=\"font-semibold\">\n                {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n        {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":771,"cache_read_input_tokens":44217,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":771},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"363d12fc-c46a-47cb-877b-d72987e759e8","uuid":"26f4a2ef-3891-42fd-9d39-61f608997fa7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:31:54.162Z","request_id":"req_011CdM86aV6s8rwkVvjhbKXo"}

[]).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}","       </div>","     </div>","   );"]}],"originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\nconst USDC = deployedContracts[8453].USDC;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","userModified":false}}

[]).map(payout => (\n          <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}`} className=\"card bg-base-200 p-3\">","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// USDC uses 6 decimals\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: sendPayout, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Payouts\" });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // parseUnits throws on malformed input (e.g. \"1.2.3\"), so guard it\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = parsedAmount !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await approveUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount] });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await sendPayout({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n      <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n      <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n      {!isConnected ? (\n        <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <p className=\"mb-4\">\n            Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n          </p>\n\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={recipient}\n            onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <textarea\n            placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n            value={memo}\n            onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n          />\n\n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={isApproving || !needsApproval} onClick={handleApprove}>\n              {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Approve\"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n              disabled={isPaying || needsApproval || !hasValidAmount}\n              onClick={handlePay}\n            >\n              {isPaying ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Send Payout\"}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n          {needsApproval && (\n            <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mt-2\">Approve USDC spending before sending this payout.</p>\n          )}\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n        {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n          <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"card bg-base-200 p-3\">\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1\">\n              <Address address={payout.args.from} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n              <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n              <Address address={payout.args.to} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n              <span className=\"font-semibold\">\n                {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n        {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":133,"oldLines":8,"newStart":133,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ","       <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>","       <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">","-        {(payouts ?? args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Payouts\" });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // parseUnits throws on malformed input (e.g. \"1.2.3\"), so guard it\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, return;\n    }\n    await approveUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount] });\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await sendPayout({ functionName: \"pay\", (\n        <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <p className=\"mb-4\">\n            Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n          </p>\n\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={recipient}\n            onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <textarea\n            placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n            value={memo}\n            onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n          />\n\n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={isApproving || !needsApproval} onClick={handleApprove}>\n              {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Approve\"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n              disabled={isPaying || needsApproval || !hasValidAmount}\n              onClick={handlePay}\n            >\n              {isPaying ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Send Payout\"}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n          {needsApproval && (\n            <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mt-2\">Approve USDC spending before sending this payout.</p>\n          )}\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n        {(payouts ?? \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // parseUnits throws on malformed input (e.g. \"1.2.3\"), so guard it\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = parsedAmount !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await approveUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await sendPayout({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n      <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n      <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n      {!isConnected ? (\n        <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <p className=\"mb-4\">\n            Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n          </p>\n\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={recipient}\n            onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <textarea\n            placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n            value={memo}\n            onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n          />\n\n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={isApproving || !needsApproval} onClick={handleApprove}>\n              {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Approve\"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n              disabled={isPaying || needsApproval || !hasValidAmount}\n              onClick={handlePay}\n            >\n              {isPaying ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Send Payout\"}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n          {needsApproval && (\n            <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mt-2\">Approve USDC spending before sending this payout.</p>\n          )}\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n        {(payouts ?? client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS_ADDRESS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts.address;\n\n// USDC uses 6 decimals\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS_ADDRESS],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: sendPayout, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Payouts\" });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // parseUnits throws on malformed input (e.g. \"1.2.3\"), so guard it\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | undefined;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = undefined;\n  }\n\n  const hasValidAmount = parsedAmount !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasValidAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await approveUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS_ADDRESS, parsedAmount] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!hasValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    await sendPayout({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n    await Promise.all([refetchBalance(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n      <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n      <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n      {!isConnected ? (\n        <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <p className=\"mb-4\">\n            Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n          </p>\n\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={recipient}\n            onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          <textarea\n            placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n            value={memo}\n            onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n          />\n\n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={isApproving || !needsApproval} onClick={handleApprove}>\n              {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Approve\"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n              disabled={isPaying || needsApproval || !hasValidAmount}\n              onClick={handlePay}\n            >\n              {isPaying ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Send Payout\"}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n          {needsApproval && (\n            <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mt-2\">Approve USDC spending before sending this payout.</p>\n          )}\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n        {(payouts ?? []).map(payout => (\n          <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}`} className=\"card bg-base-200 p-3\">\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1\">\n              <Address address={payout.args.from} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n              <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n              <Address address={payout.args.to} size=\"xs\" onlyEnsOrAddress />\n              <span className=\"font-semibold\">\n                {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : \"0\"} USDC\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n        {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":158,"startLine":1,"totalLines":158}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM8eX9KgVwrTbQ3g4Lu3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Committed cleanly (pre-commit lint-staged + typecheck passed). The final pass is done.\n\n## What I found and fixed\n\nThe Payouts contract is fine and already deployed — I left it untouched. All the real problems were in the frontend, in `/payouts` and its contract config:\n\n**1. Broken \"Latest payouts\" feed (the main shipping blocker)**\nThe feed passed `fromBlock: 0n`, forcing a full scan of Base mainnet (~33M blocks) on every load via the shared public Alchemy key — that times out / hits rate limits and the feed never renders. Removed it so the hook uses `deployedOnBlock` (33712450, the deploy block).\n\n**2. No transaction feedback — dangerous for a money app**\nWrites used raw `useWriteContract` and swallowed errors into `console.error`. Users sending USDC got no success/failure signal. Switched both approve and pay to `useScaffoldWriteContract`, which gives built-in tx toasts, error parsing, and a wrong-network guard.\n\n**3. No input validation**\nAn empty/malformed recipient or amount just threw silently. Added `isAddress` + amount/balance checks with `notification.error`, guarded `parseUnits` against malformed input, and gated the Send button on on-chain allowance (with a \"needs approval\" hint). Balance/allowance refetch right after each tx so the UI doesn't lag 30s behind the poll.\n\n**4. USDC in the wrong file (latent breakage)**\nUSDC was hand-added to the autogenerated `deployedContracts.ts`, which warns it can be overwritten. Since USDC isn't deployed by the script, the next `yarn deploy` regen would wipe it and break the app. Moved it to `externalContracts.ts`; the hooks merge both sources, so nothing else changed.\n\n**5. Theme consistency**\nPage hardcoded `bg-black text-white`, clashing with the rest of the themed SE-2 app. Converted to DaisyUI theme classes and used the `Address` component (ENS + explorer links) for the feed.\n\nVerified with `next:check-types`, `lint`, and a full production `next:build` — all green. The diff is scoped to three files (I reverted unrelated `yarn format` whitespace churn and build-regenerated artifacts).\n\nOne thing I did **not** change, worth flagging: `scaffold.config.ts` ships the shared public Alchemy key and WalletConnect project ID as fallbacks. They're env-overridable (`NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID`), so before real users hit it, set your own keys in the Vercel/prod env to avoid rate limits."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":981,"cache_read_input_tokens":74511,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":981},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"363d12fc-c46a-47cb-877b-d72987e759e8","uuid":"dbafc51b-69f4-4bae-8173-9181eb02b1b0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:38:53.356Z","request_id":"req_011CdM8eVXqTamG3MHDTvi1F"}
